Let's implement own C computation efficiently. For each lag k loop as before. n up to maybe 1000, m=1999, total terms ~n^2/2=500k for n=1000. Python loop with numpy slices: m ~1000, each slice sum. 1000* sum vector length ~500k elements, fast. Could do:
```
def make_c_ours(h,n):
    m=2*n-1
    C=np.zeros(m,float)
    for k in range(-(n-1), n):
       idx=n-1+k
       nv=n-abs(k)
       if k>=0:
          a=h[:nv]; b=h[k:k+nv]
       else:
          kk=-k; a=h[kk:]; b=h[:nv]
       C[idx]=np.dot(a, 1.0-b) # counts
    return C
```
This is O(n^2) and for n=1000, 1000 dot of 500 =500k mult, fine (milliseconds). It should match our A orientation: c_k = ∑ h_a(1-h_{a+k}) where for k>=0 a=0..nv-1; for k<0 a=kk..n-1 (a+k=0..nv-1). In code above for k<0: a=h[kk:], b=h[:nv] (b = h[a+k] yes). Good.

Now build A for C_ours. For each lag k, coefficient derivative wrt h_i:
C_k = ∑_{a:a+k valid} h_a (1-h_{a+k}). Differentiate wrt h_i:
- if i is first a (i+k valid): + (1 - h_{i+k})
- if i is second b, b = a+k => a=i-k valid: includes h_{a}(1-h_i), derivative -h_{i-k}.
Thus:
```
A[i, idx] += (1 - h[i+k]) if 0<=i+k<n
A[i, idx] -= h[i-k] if 0<=i-k<n
```
Build loop over k:
```
A = zeros((n,m))
for k in ...:
 idx=...
 if k>=0:
   nv=n-k
   a=np.arange(nv)
   A[a, idx] += 1 - h[a+k]
   b=np.arange(k,n)
   A[b, idx] -= h[b-k]
 else:
   kk=-k; nv=n-kk
   a=np.arange(kk,n)
   A[a, idx] += 1 - h[a-kk]  # i+k = i-kk valid
   b=np.arange(0,nv)
   A[b, idx] -= h[b+kk]
```
For k=0: first A +=1-h; second A -=h => 1-2h. Good.

Compute C_ours:
```
def compute_C_ours(h,n):
    m=2*n-1
    C=np.empty(m)
    idx=n-1
    C[idx]=np.dot(h,1-h)
    for k in range(1,n):
        nv=n-k
        a=h[:nv]; b=h[k:k+nv]
        v=np.dot(a, 1.0-b)
        C[idx+k]=v
        # negative? c_{-k}=dot(h[k:],1-h[:nv])
        C[idx-k]=np.dot(h[k:], 1.0-h[:nv])
    return C
```
This uses two dots per k: total n^2. For n=500 -> 250k? Actually positive+negative ~2*sum(n-k)= n(n-1) ~250k elements, good.

def try_pair_move(C,W,A,h,i,j,d,n):
    W[...] = C
    W += d*(A[i]-A[j])
    base=n-1
    W[base] += -2*d*d
    if i!=j:
      if 0< j-i < n: W[base+j-i] += d*d
      if 0< i-j < n: W[base+i-j] += d*d
    v=W.max()*? (counts; no dx for comparison)
    if accept: C=W swap; h[i]+=d; h[j]-=d; update A.
```

Pseudo:
```
def binary_local_best(C,A,h,n,deadline, max_sweeps=100, tol=1e-13):
    best_count = C.max()
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time>deadline: break
       h = np.asarray(h)
       zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
       ones  = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]
       if len(zeros)==0 or len(ones)==0: break
       bi=-1; bj=-1; bv=best_count
       # To reduce iterations, maybe randomly shuffle? Deterministic.
       # Optimize: choose smaller list as outer? zeros and ones similar.
       for i in zeros:
          Ai=A[i]
          # If i is now 1? h changed only during sweep if we accept at end, so h stable.
          for j in ones:
             W = C + Ai - A[j]
             W[base]-=2
             if j>i: W[base+j-i]+=1
             else: W[base+i-j]+=1
             v=W.max()
             if v < bv - 1e-12:
                 bv=v; bi=i; bj=j
       if bi>=0:
          # apply move using exact delta maybe recompute to avoid stale? Use same formula d=1
          C += A[bi] - A[bj]; C[base]-=2; ...
          h[bi]+=1; h[bj]-=1
          update_A(A,bi,+1,n); update_A(A,bj,-1,n)
          best_count=bv
          improved=True
       else: break
    return C,A,h,best_count
```

Line search exact for pair d:
```
def eval_pair_move(C,W,A,i,j,d,n,base):
    np.copyto(W,C)
    W += d*A[i]
    W -= d*A[j]
    W[base] += -2*d*d
    if j>i: W[base+j-i] += d*d
    else: W[base+i-j] += d*d
    return float(W.max())
```
This does 2 multiplies? `d*A[i]` temp. Could use `np.multiply(A[i], d, out=tmp); W += tmp`. We can have global tmp. But function top-level can't easily access unless pass. Simpler, use `W += d * A[i]` (alloc). Fine for limited line search.

`continuous_local` loop:
```
def continuous_local(C,A,h,n, deadline, max_iters=2000, tol=1e-12):
    bestc=C.max()
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time>deadline: break
       maxc = C.max()
       if bestc - maxc > 1e-12: bestc=maxc
       active_count = min(100, 2*n-1)
       active = np.argsort(C)[-active_count:]  # largest values
       # maybe ensure unique? argsort returns unique.
       Aact = A[:, active]  # n x L
       Cact = C[active]
       max_inactive = np.max(np.delete(C, active)) if len(active)<len(C) else -inf # costly. Use top outside approx: We can just include current max in active.
       # For i in range(n): if 1-h_i > 1e-12
       cand=[]
       # To avoid equal pairs, require h_j > h_i + 1e-12 or i==? Also D>0.
       # But if h_i==h_j, moving mass could be useful; skip to avoid symmetric duplicate.
       for i in range(n):
           room_gain=1.0-h[i]
           if room_gain <=1e-12: continue
           js = np.nonzero(h > h[i] + 1e-12)[0]
           if js.size==0: continue
           Js=h[js]
           D = np.minimum(room_gain, Js)
           # maybe score D small
           dsc = np.minimum(0.05, D)
           # B = Aact[:,i] - Aact[:,js] shape L,J
           B = Aact[:, i:i+1] - Aact[:, js]  # broadcasting? Aact[:,i,i+1] shape L,1; Aact[:,js] L,J => L,J
           scores = np.max(Cact[None,:] + dsc * B, axis=0)
           # add current inactive? keep simple
           # store top few per i
           order=np.argsort(scores)[:min(3, js.size)]
           for t in order:
               jj=js[t]
               Dval=float(D[t])
               if Dval>1e-12:
                   cand.append((float(scores[t]), i, jj, Dval))
       if not cand: break
       cand.sort()
       # take top K unique
       prev=set; bestimprovement=0; bestinfo=None
       for score,i,j,Dval in cand:
           if (i,j) in prev: continue; prev.add((i,j))
           # line search
           vals = [Dval, Dval*0.5, Dval*0.25, Dval*0.125, Dval*0.03125, min(0.01,Dval), min(0.001,Dval)]
           # maybe also d = Dval*0.8
           bestv=maxc; bestd=None
           for d in vals:
              if d<=1e-14 or d>Dval+1e-12: continue
              v=eval_pair(C,W,A,i,j,d,n,base)
              if v < bestv - 1e-12: bestv=v; bestd=d
           if bestd is not None and bestv < bestc - 1e-10:
               apply_pair(...)
               bestc=bestv
               improved=True
               # after first accept, break to recompute active
               break
       if not improved: break
    return C,A,h,bestc
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

def eval_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')) * (2.0/n))

def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(h.copy(),0.0,1.0)
    target=float(n//2 if n%2==0 else (n+1)//2?) Need target n/2 maybe not integer if n odd. Problem requires sum n/2 exactly, so n even. Use target=n/2.
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h += diff/tot*room
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h += diff/tot*h
    # adjust tiny residual
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       # find interior index far from 0/1
       if diff>0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
          add=min(diff, 1.0-h[idx]); h[idx]+=add
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(h))
          add=max(diff, -h[idx]); h[idx]+=add
    return h
```
This projection may not preserve exact if n odd; but n even. Good.

`compute_C_ours`:
```
def compute_C_ours(h,n):
    m=2*n-1; base=n-1; C=np.zeros(m)
    C[base]=np.dot(h,1-h)
    for k in range(1,n):
       nv=n-k
       C[base+k]=np.dot(h[:nv], 1-h[k:k+nv])
       C[base-k]=np.dot(h[k:], 1-h[:nv])
    return C
```
For n=1? not.

`build_A`:
```
def build_A(h,n):
    m=2*n-1; base=n-1; A=np.zeros((n,m))
    A[:,base]=1.0-2.0*h
    for k in range(1,n):
       nv=n-k
       ip=base+k; ineg=base-k
       A[:nv,ip] += 1.0 - h[k:kk+nv]
       A[kk:,ip] -= h[:nv]
       A[kk:,ineg] += 1.0 - h[:nv]
       A[:nv,ineg] -= h[kk:]
    return A
```
Need check negative: For k=1, n=3, idxn base-1. A[kk:, ineg] +=1-h[:nv] where kk=1,nv=2. Rows1,2 values h0,h1. Negative k=-1: C= h1(1-h0)+h2(1-h1). Coeff row1: 1-h0 + second? row1-l = row1-(-1)=2? -h2? Wait general for k=-1: coefficient row1: first if 1-1=0 valid 1-h0; second if 1-(-1)=2 valid -h2. Our code row1 +=1-h0; also row1 in second? A[:nv] subtract h[kk:] where rows0,1 subtract h1,h2. So row1 subtract h2. Good. row2 +=1-h1; second row2? p-l=3 invalid. row0: first invalid (0-1), second p-l=1 valid -h1; code row0 subtract h1? A[:2] subtract h[1:] both row0 h1, row1 h2. Good.

`update_A_change`:
```
def update_A_change(A, q, delta, rows, base):
    if delta == 0: return
    cols = base + q - rows
    A[rows, cols] -= delta
    A[rows, base + rows - q] -= delta
```
Need `rows` precomputed int array.

`apply_pair`:
```
def apply_pair(C,A,h,i,j,d,rows,base):
    if abs(d)<1e-15: return 0? returns
    C += d*A[i]
    C -= d*A[j]
    C[base] += -2*d*d
    if i != j:
       if j > i: C[base + j - i] += d*d
       else: C[base + i - j] += d*d
    h[i] += d; h[j] -= d
    update_A_change(A, i, d, rows, base)
    update_A_change(A, j, -d, rows, base)
    # clip? Maybe return new max
    return float(C.max())
```
Use d*d.

`eval_pair_move(C,W,A,i,j,d,base)`:
```
def eval_pair_move(C,W,A,i,j,d,base):
    np.copyto(W,C)
    if d != 1.0:
       W += d*A[i]
       W -= d*A[j]
    else:
       W += A[i]
       W -= A[j]
    W[base] += -2.0*d*d
    if j>i: W[base+j-i] += d*d
    elif i>j: W[base+i-j] += d*d
    return float(W.max())
```
This alloc temp for `d*A[i]` if d not 1. For binary d=1 no. Good.

`best_binary_move(C,W,A,h,n,rows,base,deadline)`:
```
def binary_local(C,A,h,n,rows,base,deadline, max_sweeps=100, W=None):
    if W is None: W=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    bestc=float(C.max())
    improved=True
    sweeps=0
    while improved and sweeps<max_sweeps and time.time()<deadline:
       improved=False; sweeps+=1
       zero=np.nonzero(h<0.5)[0]
       one=np.nonzero(h>0.5)[0]
       if zero.size==0 or one.size==0: break
       bi=-1; bj=-1; bv=bestc
       # Maybe randomize? deterministic.
       # Optimize by outer smaller? zero and one equal.
       for i in zero:
           Ai=A[i]
           # local check if h[i] changed? h not modified during scan.
           for j in one:
               np.copyto(W,C)
               W += Ai
               W -= A[j]
               W[base] += -2.0
               if j>i: W[base+j-i] += 1.0
               else: W[base+i-j] += 1.0
               v=float(W.max())
               if v < bv - 1e-12:
                   bv=v; bi=i; bj=j
       if bi>=0:
           bestc = apply_pair(C,A,h,bi,bj,1.0,rows,base)
           improved=True
    return bestc
```
This scans all pairs. If n=1000, zero*one=250k, W ops length1999 => 500M ops per sweep too slow. Need improve for larger n. For n=500, 62k*1000=62M per sweep, maybe okay but 100 sweeps 6B ops maybe high. We need limit sweeps or optimize. Let's estimate more realistically: 62M float ops in numpy C ~0.03s? Python 62k loop with 4 ufuncs maybe 0.5s. 100 sweeps 50s. okay. If n=800, zero*one=160k, vector len1599 ->256M ops per sweep, maybe 2s; 20 sweeps 40s. Acceptable. We'll cap binary sweeps based on n: `max_sweeps = max(10, int(200000 // n))?` Or time. Use while time.
Can optimize by using active score first? But binary local all-pairs may be okay for n=500. Use `max_sweeps = 80` and deadline.
Potential issue: `W += Ai; W -= A[j]` modifies W; after scan, W has last candidate. copyto resets. Good.

We can improve scanning by selecting outer list smaller (frac) but both equal. Could use `if zero.size > one.size: swap?` But direction is zero gain, one lose; can't swap. Need all pairs.

`continuous_local` code. Need W. It will use C,max. Let's write efficient:
```
def continuous_local(C,A,h,n,base,rows,W,deadline, max_iters=200, L=80):
    bestc=float(C.max())
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       maxc=float(C.max())
       if maxc < bestc - 1e-12: bestc=maxc
       if maxc > bestc + 1e-12: bestc=maxc  # shouldn't
       active = np.argsort(C)[-L:]
       Aact = A[:, active]
       Cact = C[active]
       cand=[]
       # maybe if parent nearly binary and no interior, continuous not needed? But still.
       H=h
       # precompute js for each i? loop
       for i in range(n):
           room=1.0-H[i]
           if room <= 1e-12: continue
           # Use threshold: j where H[j] > H[i] + 1e-10
           high_mask = H > H[i] + 1e-10
           if not np.any(high_mask): continue
           js = np.nonzero(high_mask)[0]
           D = np.minimum(room, H[js])
           # if all D <= 1e-10 skip
           dsc = np.minimum(0.05, D)
           # maybe also dsc = np.maximum(dsc, 1e-4)? no.
           B = Aact[:, i] - Aact[:, js]  # shape L x J? Aact[:,i] (L,), Aact[:,js] (L,J) broadcasting ok.
           # scores = max(Cact[None,:] + dsc[None?]*B?) dsc shape J, need broadcast along L: Cact[None,:] + B * dsc[None,:]
           scores = np.max(Cact[None, :] + B * dsc[None, :], axis=0)
           # store top 2 per i
           top = np.argpartition(scores, min(2,scores.size)-1)[:2] if scores.size>2 else np.argsort(scores)[:2]
           for tt in top:
               jj = js[tt]
               Dval = float(D[tt])
               if Dval > 1e-10:
                   cand.append((float(scores[tt]), i, jj, Dval))
       if not cand: break
       cand.sort(key=lambda x: x[0]) # lambda not allowed? "No closures or lambdas" in problem. Need avoid lambda. Use tuple sort default sorts by first. cand list tuples (score,i,j,D); default sort works.
       local_best = False
       for score,i,j,Dval in cand:
           if time.time()>deadline: break
           # line search values
           tries = (Dval, 0.5*Dval, 0.25*Dval, 0.125*Dval, min(0.05, Dval), min(0.01, Dval), min(0.001, Dval))
           bestd=0.0; bestv=float('inf')
           # use current maxc as initial? We need compare to maxc. If a sample doesn't improve, skip. Set bestv=maxc.
           bestv=maxc
           for d in tries:
               if d <= 1e-12 or d > Dval + 1e-12: continue
               v = eval_pair_move(C,W,A,i,j,float(d),base)
               if v < bestv - 1e-12:
                   bestv=v; bestd=float(d)
           if bestd > 0 and bestv < maxc - 1e-10:
               apply_pair(C,A,h,i,j,bestd,rows,base)
               local_best=True
               bestc = float(C.max())
               break
       if not local_best:
           # maybe try random pairs if no active good
           # Could break to avoid loop
           break
    return bestc

Let's write `subgradient_polish(h,n,deadline, iters=80)`:
```
def subgradient_polish(h,n,deadline, alphas=(50,200,1000,5000,20000)):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); bestc=eval_c5(h,n)
    C=compute_C_ours(h,n); A=build_A(h,n)
    dx=2.0/n
    # We can use C counts smooth value.
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time>deadline: break
       for it in range(30):
          if time>deadline: break
          Cm=C.max()
          z=alpha*(C-Cm); z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum()
          grad=dx*(A @ w)
          # move toward reducing smooth obj; also maybe add small equality? projection handles
          accepted=False
          for step in (0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002):
              hn=project_feasible(h - step*grad, n)
              cn=eval_c5(hn,n)
              if cn < bestc - 1e-12:
                  h=hn; bestc=cn; accepted=True; recompute C,A? 
                  break
          if not accepted: break
          # after accept, recompute C,A maybe if step large
          C=compute_C_ours(h,n); A=build_A(h,n)
    return h,bestc
```

Evaluation function:
```
def line_eval(h,v,lam,n):
    hn = h + lam*v
    # if all within, compute C_own max; else project
    if np.min(hn)<-1e-10 or np.max(hn)>1+1e-10: hn=project_feasible(hn,n)
    return compute_C_ours(hn,n).max()*dx, hn
```

Detailed active_lp_polish:
```
def active_lp_polish(h,n,deadline, max_iters=20, L=40, dx=None):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); bestc=eval_c5(h,n)
    C=compute_C_ours(h,n)
    A=build_A(h,n)
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time>deadline: break
       maxc = float(C.max())
       # if maxc smaller than bestc, update? C synchronized
       if maxc < bestc - 1e-12: bestc=maxc* (2/n)? Wait C counts, bestc objective. Maintain bestc_obj.
       active = np.argsort(C)[-L:]
       # if active constraint matrix rank? solve
       try:
           Arows = A[:, active].T  # L x n
           A_ub = np.hstack([Arows, -np.ones((L,1))])
           b_ub = np.zeros(L)
           A_eq = np.hstack([np.ones(n), 0.0])
           bounds = [( -h[i], 1.0-h[i]) for i in range(n)] + [(None, 0.0)]
           res = linprog(c=np.concatenate([np.zeros(n), [1.0]]), A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=0.0, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
       except Exception:
           break
       if res is None or res.status != 0: break
       tval = float(res.x[-1])
       if tval > -1e-9: break  # no active first-order improvement
       v = res.x[:n]
       # If norm small break?
       if np.linalg.norm(v) < 1e-12: break
       # line search
       lam_max=1.0
       pos = v > 1e-10; neg = v < -1e-10
       if pos.any(): lam_max = min(lam_max, np.min((1-h[pos] - 1e-12)/v[pos]))
       if neg.any(): lam_max = min(lam_max, np.min((h[neg] - 1e-12)/(-v[neg])))
       lam_max = min(lam_max, 0.999)
       if lam_max <= 1e-6: break
       # sample lams
       lams = np.array([0.0, 0.01*lam_max, 0.02*lam_max, 0.05*lam_max, 0.1*lam_max, 0.2*lam_max, 0.3*lam_max, 0.5*lam_max, 0.7*lam_max, 0.9*lam_max])
       lams = np.unique(np.clip(lams,0,lam_max))
       # add linspace 20
       lams = np.unique(np.concatenate((lams, np.linspace(0.0, lam_max, 21))))
       best_lam=0.0; best_val=maxc; best_hn=None
       # skip lam0
       for lam in lams:
           if lam<=1e-12: continue
           hn = h + lam*v
           # numeric clip/project only if needed
           if hn.min() < -1e-10 or hn.max() > 1+1e-10:
               hn = project_feasible(hn,n)
           Cn = compute_C_ours(hn,n)
           val = float(Cn.max())
           if val < best_val - 1e-12:
               best_val=val; best_lam=float(lam); best_hn=hn
       # refine around best_lam
       if best_lam>0:
           half = max(0.1*lam_max, abs(best_lam)*0.2)
           extra = np.linspace(max(0,best_lam-half), min(lam_max,best_lam+half), 21)
           ...
       if best_hn is not None:
           h=best_hn; C=compute_C_ours(h,n); A=build_A(h,n); maxc=best_val
           c_obj = maxc*2/n
           if c_obj < bestc_obj - 1e-12: bestc_obj=c_obj
       else:
           # try next active set maybe expand L? if no line improvement but LP says t<0, maybe exact inactive blocks at all λ; break or increase L?
           # Could try larger L and again; if still none break.
           # We'll break to avoid loop.
           break
    # final project/eval
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    bestc=eval_c5(h,n)
    return h,bestc
```

Let's write code carefully.

```
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import linprog

BASE_TOL = 1e-12

def eval_c5(h,n):
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full'))*dx)

def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target=0.5*len(h)
    for _ in range(200):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room=1.0-h
            tot=room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15: break
            h += (diff/tot)*room
        else:
            tot=h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15: break
            h += (diff/tot)*h
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
        if diff>0:
            idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
            h[idx]=min(1.0,h[idx]+diff)
        else:
            idx=int(np.argmax(h))
            h[idx]=max(0.0,h[idx]+diff)
    return h
```
Good.

`compute_C_ours` and `build_A` as above.

`update_A_change` uses rows and base. If delta tiny skip.

```
def update_A_change(A,q,delta,rows,base):
    if delta == 0.0: return
    A[rows, base+q-rows] -= delta
    A[rows, base+rows-q] -= delta
```
Need `rows` dtype int. `base+q-rows` int array.

`apply_pair`:
```
def apply_pair(C,A,h,i,j,d,rows,base):
    if d == 0.0: return float(C.max())
    C += d*A[i]
    C -= d*A[j]
    d2=d*d
    C[base] += -2.0*d2
    if i != j:
        if j > i: C[base+j-i] += d2
        else: C[base+i-j] += d2
    hi=float(h[i])+d; hj=float(h[j])-d
    if hi < 0 and hi > -1e-12: hi=0.0
    elif hi > 1 and hi < 1+1e-12: hi=1.0
    if hj < 0 and hj > -1e-12: hj=0.0
    elif hj > 1 and hj < 1+1e-12: hj=1.0
    h[i]=hi; h[j]=hj
    update_A_change(A,i,d,rows,base)
    update_A_change(A,j,-d,rows,base)
    return float(C.max())
```
If clipping changes sum by > tol? Not.

`eval_pair_move` as above. Use `copyto`.

`binary_local`:
Need pass W. We'll implement deadline and max_sweeps. Use local variables for speed:
```
def binary_local(C,A,h,n,rows,base,W,deadline,max_sweeps=100):
    bestc=float(C.max())
    sweeps=0
    improved=True
    while improved and sweeps<max_sweeps and time.time()<deadline:
        improved=False
        sweeps +=1
        zero=np.nonzero(h <= 1e-9)[0]
        one=np.nonzero(h >= 1.0-1e-9)[0]
        if zero.size==0 or one.size==0:
            break
        bi=-1; bj=-1; bv=bestc
        # To speed, maybe choose if zero/one length large and deadline close? skip
        for ii in range(zero.size):
            i=int(zero[ii])
            Ai=A[i]
            # if h changed? no.
            for jj in range(one.size):
                j=int(one[jj])
                np.copyto(W,C)
                W += Ai
                W -= A[j]
                W[base] += -2.0
                if j > i:
                    W[base+j-i] += 1.0
                else:
                    W[base+i-j] += 1.0
                v=float(W.max())
                if v < bv - 1e-11:
                    bv=v; bi=i; bj=j
        if bi >=0:
            bestc=apply_pair(C,A,h,bi,bj,1.0,rows,base)
            if bestc < bv - 1e-8: # if mismatch due to apply exact? keep bestc
                bv=bestc
            improved=True
    return bestc
```
Could optimize by precomputing one list of A? no.

If n large, this loop may be too slow. We can add a fast rejection: before full W operations, approximate using current max and A at active lags? But code complexity. For n<=600 okay. We'll limit binary phase to n<=700 maybe; for larger, use fewer sweeps/active subset? If best_n >700, skip full binary or use active scoring. We can check in run.

`continuous_local` as above. Need W. Let's implement with no lambda.

```
def continuous_local(C,A,h,n,rows,base,W,deadline,max_iters=200,L=80):
    dx? not needed, C counts.
    bestc=float(C.max())
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        maxc=float(C.max())
        if maxc < bestc - 1e-12: bestc=maxc
        active=np.argsort(C)[-L:]
        Aact=A[:, active]
        Cact=C[active]
        cand=[]
        H=h
        for i in range(n):
            room=1.0-H[i]
            if room <= 1e-12: continue
            high=H > H[i] + 1e-10
            if not np.any(high): continue
            js=np.nonzero(high)[0]
            D=np.minimum(room, H[js])
            mask=D>1e-10
            if not np.any(mask): continue
            js=js[mask]; D=D[mask]
            dsc=np.minimum(0.05, D)
            B=Aact[:, i] - Aact[:, js]
            scores=np.max(Cact[None, :] + B * dsc[None, :], axis=0)
            k=min(3, len(scores))
            order=np.argsort(scores)[:k]
            for t in order:
                if D[t] > 1e-10:
                    cand.append((float(scores[t]), int(i), int(js[t]), float(D[t])))
        if not cand: break
        cand.sort()
        local_best=False
        # limit candidates to e.g., 200 to avoid long line search
        tested=0
        for score,i,j,Dval in cand:
            if time.time()>deadline or tested>=200: break
            tested +=1
            tries=(Dval, 0.5*Dval, 0.25*Dval, 0.125*Dval, min(0.05,Dval), min(0.01,Dval), min(0.001,Dval))
            bestv=maxc; bestd=0.0
            for d in tries:
                if d <= 1e-12 or d > Dval + 1e-12: continue
                v=eval_pair_move(C,W,A,i,j,float(d),base)
                if v < bestv - 1e-11:
                    bestv=v; bestd=float(d)
            if bestd > 0.0 and bestv < maxc - 1e-9:
                apply_pair(C,A,h,i,j,bestd,rows,base)
                bestc=float(C.max())
                local_best=True
                cand=None
                break
        if not local_best:
            break
    return bestc
```
Potential memory: `B=Aact[:, i] - Aact[:, js]` if js large 500, L 80 =>40k. Fine.
But `cand.sort()` sorts by score; if scores all similar, okay. It may pick pair with huge D but not best. Exact line search accepts only improvement. If none, stops. Could be too quick. Maybe if no improvement due to line search samples missing, but LP phase might help.

`active_lp_polish`:
Need use C counts for line search. We can compute objective count = C.max(). bestc count; at end convert. But run best objective uses np. We'll return h,best_count? Simpler return h,best_obj.
```
def active_lp_polish(h,n,deadline,max_iters=30,L_choices=(40,80,160),sample_points=25):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    dx=2.0/n
    best_obj=eval_c5(h,n)
    C=compute_C_ours(h,n)
    A=build_A(h,n)
    count_best=float(C.max())
    it=0
    for L in L_choices:
       for it2 in range(max_iters):
          if time.time()>deadline: return h,best_obj
          Luse=min(L,2*n-1)
          active=np.argsort(C)[-Luse:]
          # if all at max? ok
          Arows=A[:, active].T
          try:
             m=2*n-1
             A_ub=np.empty((len(active), n+1))
             A_ub[:,:n]=Arows
             A_ub[:,n]=-1.0
             b_ub=np.zeros(len(active))
             A_eq=np.zeros(1,n+1); A_eq[0,:n]=1.0
             bounds=[]
             for val in h:
                lower=-1.0*float(val)
                upper=1.0-float(val)
                if upper < lower: upper=lower
                bounds.append((lower,upper))
             bounds.append((-1000.0,0.0))
             c=np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1.0
             res=linprog(c,A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=b_ub,A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=0.0,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'presolve':True})
          except Exception:
             break
          if res is None or res.status !=0: break
          tval=float(res.x[-1])
          if tval > -1e-8: break
          v=res.x[:n].astype(float)
          if np.linalg.norm(v) < 1e-12: break
          lam_max=0.999
          pos=v>1e-12
          if np.any(pos):
              ratios=(1.0-h[pos]-1e-12)/v[pos]
              lam_max=min(lam_max, float(np.min(ratios)))
          neg=v<-1e-12
          if np.any(neg):
              ratios=(h[neg]-1e-12)/(-v[neg])
              lam_max=min(lam_max, float(np.min(ratios)))
          if lam_max <=1e-6: break
          # sample
          lams=np.linspace(0.0,lam_max,24)
          lams=np.concatenate((lams, np.array([0.01*lam_max,0.02*lam_max,0.05*lam_max,0.1*lam_max,0.5*lam_max])))
          lams=np.unique(np.clip(lams,0.0,lam_max))
          maxc=float(C.max())
          best_lam=0.0; best_val=maxc; best_hn=None
          for lam in lams:
             if lam <=1e-12: continue
             hn=h+float(lam)*v
             if hn.min() < -1e-10 or hn.max() > 1.0+1e-10:
                 hn=project_feasible(hn,n)
             Cn=compute_C_ours(hn,n)
             val=float(Cn.max())
             if val < best_val - 1e-10:
                 best_val=val; best_lam=float(lam); best_hn=hn.copy()
          if best_lam >0:
             half=max(0.02*lam_max, abs(best_lam)*0.25)
             extra=np.linspace(max(0.0,best_lam-half), min(lam_max,best_lam+half),17)
             for lam in extra:
                if lam<=1e-12 or (lam>best_lam+half and False): continue
                hn=h+float(lam)*v
                if hn.min() ... project
                val=compute...
                if val < best_val - 1e-10: ...
          if best_hn is not None:
             h=best_hn
             C=compute_C_ours(h,n)
             A=build_A(h,n)
             count_best=float(C.max())
             obj=count_best*dx
             # eval exact np maybe slower but do for best_obj
             obj=eval_c5(h,n)
             if obj < best_obj - 1e-14: best_obj=obj
             last_improved=it2+1
          else:
             # maybe try next active L; break inner
             break
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_obj=eval_c5(h,n)
    return h,best_obj
```

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+max(1,budget_s)-4
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h_init=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       n_init=len(h_init)
       if n_init %2 ==1: # make even? maybe trim?
           if n_init>0: h_init=h_init[:-1]; n_init-=1
    except Exception:

       # random binary init
       h_init=np.zeros(n_init); idx=rng.choice(n_init,n_init//2,replace=False); h_init[idx]=1
    n_init=int(n_init)
    h=project_feasible(h_init,n_init)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_n=n_init; best_c=eval_c5(best_h,best_n)
    rows_all=[]? We'll create for each n.
    # phase 1 active LP polish on best_n
    if best_n <= 850 and time.time() < deadline-300:
       dl=min(deadline-300, t0+0.45*(budget_s-5))
       h1,c1=active_lp_polish(best_h,best_n,dl)
       if c1<best_c: best_h=h1; best_c=c1
    # phase 2 continuous pair on best_n
    if best_n <= 850 and time.time() < deadline-200:
       h_work=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
       C=compute_C_ours(h_work,best_n); A=build_A(h_work,best_n); rows=np.arange(best_n); base=best_n-1; W=np.zeros(2*best_n-1)
       dl=min(deadline-200, time.time()+0.4*(budget_s-5)) # maybe 400s? Better: t0+0.65*budget
       continuous_local(C,A,h_work,best_n,rows,base,W,dl)
       h_out=project_feasible(h_work,best_n); c_out=eval_c5(h_out,best_n)
       if c_out<best_c: best_h=h_out; best_c=c_out
    # phase 3 binary local on threshold of best
    if best_n <= 700 and time.time() < deadline-150:
       h_bin=make_binary(best_h,best_n)
       C=compute_C_ours(h_bin,best_n); A=build_A(h_bin,best_n); rows=... W...

       binary_local(C,A,h_bin,best_n,rows,base,W,dl,max_sweeps=80)
       h_out=project_feasible(h_bin,best_n); c_out=eval_c5(h_out,best_n)
       if c_out<best_c: best_h=h_out; best_n=...; best_c=c_out
    # phase 4 explore smaller/other n
    if time.time() < deadline-120:
       explored = [maybe n//2]
       for n2 in [200,250,300,400, int(1.5*best_n), int(2*best_n)]:
           if time.time() > deadline-80: break
           if n2 < 20 or n2>900 or n2%2: continue
           if n2==best_n: continue
           # create start
           x_old=np.linspace(0,2,best_n,endpoint=False); x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n2,endpoint=False)
           h2=np.interp(x_new,x_old,best_h)
           h2=project_feasible(h2,n2)
           # maybe binary and continuous
           # if n2 small, do binary local quickly from threshold and continuous
           W2... 
           # For small n, binary local can be fast; run up to small time

           c2=eval; update
           # continuous from h2
           if time...: C,A,W; continuous_local with small deadline; eval update
    # final quick polish if time >30: active LP or continuous pair small
    if time.time() < deadline-10:
       dl=deadline-2
       h1,c1=active_lp_polish(best_h,best_n,dl, max_iters=10,L_choices=(40,80))
       if c1<best_c: ...
       # final pair? maybe continuous local small
    return best_h,best_c,best_n

Now, let's refine active LP to be more effective. The LP with full box bounds and t≤0 solves:
min t s.t. A_l v ≤ t for active l.
This allows t negative arbitrarily by scaling v, but bounds limit. Since v bounds are h to 0 or 1, t might be very negative if direction can reduce active by large amount. But line search samples up to λ=1. If v has large norm, small λ may improve. The LP does not constrain the size of v relative to improvement. It may choose an extreme direction that reduces active but also mes inactive; line search maybe no overall improvement. To improve robustness, we can solve LP with additional constraint `sum of positive parts ≤ δ`? That is linear if introduce variables? Could enforce ℓ1 norm ≤ δ using two constraints? Minimize max active change subject to ||v||_1 ≤ δ and sum v=0, box. This gives a small guaranteed step and approximates best descent direction. Add variable? To constrain ℓ1 ≤ δ, need auxiliary p,q. Could just bound each v_i ≤ δ, but sum could be nδ. Or use `sum pos` if v variables? We can introduce variables pos_i, neg_i for |v_i|, but doubles variables. For n=500, okay. But not necessary.

Alternatively, after obtaining v, scale so that maximum component =0.05? But the LP direction magnitude doesn't matter; line search can choose λ small. If v extreme, λ small. The samples include 0.01 lam_max (where lam_max=1 relative to v). If v is 1.0 in some component, 0.01 step. Good.

What if v is at bounds and lam_max small due to one variable near bound? Since bounds are full box, lam_max=1 unless numerical. Good.

Line search coarse 24 points may miss optimal if F has narrow valley. We can add **golden section** on exact F if coarse best not at endpoint? But F may nonconvex. We can refine with 17 points around best. Good.

k0=0.
k=1: nv=2: h[:2]=[0,1], h[1:]=[1,0] -> dot [0,1] with [0,1]=1.

k=1: nv=2, ip=3.

A[1:,3] -=h[:2]=[0,1] -> row1:0-1=-1,row2: -1.

For k=1 positive, C1 = h0(1-h1)+h1(1-h2)=0*0 +1*1=1. Derivative wrt h0: 1-h1 =0. Derivative wrt h1: from first derivative of h1? h1 is first in second term: 1-h2=1; from second derivative of h2? h1 is second if p-l? For row1, coefficient also -h0=0. Total 1. Derivative wrt h2: -h1=-1. So A should row0=0,row1=1,row2=-1. Our code gave row0=1? Because `A[:nv, ip] += 1.0 - h[k:kk+nv]` for rows0,1 values 1-h[1]=0, 1-h[2]=1. So row0 +=0, row1 +=1. I mistakenly said [0,1]? 1-h[1:3]=1-[1,0]=[0,1]. Then `A[kk:, ip] -= h[:nv]`: rows1,2 subtract h[0],h[1] =0,1 -> row1 -0, row2 -1. Thus row0=0,row1=1,row2=-1. Good.
k=-1 ineg=1:
A[kk:, ineg] +=1-h[:nv]: rows1,2 += 1-h[0]=1,1-h[1]=0 -> row1+=1,row2+=0.

Rows:0 col2,1 col1,2 col0. second: A[rows, base+rows-0] -=1: cols2,3,4? wait m=5 base2, row2 col4. Which coefficients depend on h0? Function `make_random_binary`:
```
def make_random_binary(n,rng):
    h=np.zeros(n)
    k=n//2
    if k>0:
       idx=rng.choice(n,k,replace=False)
       h[idx]=1.0
    return h
```
`make_binary`:
```
def make_binary(h,n):
    b=np.zeros(n)
    k=n//2
    if k>0:
       idx=np.argsort(h, kind='mergesort')[::-1][:k]
       b[idx]=1.0
    return b
```
`np.argsort` for n=900 fine.

`make_start_n`? inline.

In `run`, when needing data, define local variables `rows = np.arange(n)`, `base = n-1`, `W = np.zeros(2*n-1)`. We pass.

Be careful variable names `in` reserved; use `ineg`.

`build_A` code:
```
def build_A(h,n):
    m=2*n-1; base=n-1
    A=np.zeros((n,m), dtype=float)
    A[:,base]=1.0-2.0*h
    for k in range(1,n):
       nv=n-k
       ip=base+k; ineg=base-k
       A[:nv,ip] += 1.0 - h[k:k+nv]
       A[kk:,ip] -= h[:nv]
       A[kk:,ineg] += 1.0 - h[:nv]
       A[:nv,ineg] -= h[kk:]
    return A
```
Check negative: `A[:nv, ineg] -= h[kk:]` where kk=k, h[k:k+nv]? yes h[kk:kk+nv]=$h[k:]$ length nv. Good.

`compute_C_ours`:
```
def compute_C_ours(h,n):
    m=2*n-1; base=n-1
    C=np.zeros(m)
    C[base]=np.dot(h,1.0-h)
    for k in range(1,n):
       nv=n-k
       C[base+k]=np.dot(h[:nv], 1.0-h[k:k+nv])
       C[base-k]=np.dot(h[k:], 1.0-h[:nv])
    return C
```

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float).copy(),0,1)
    t=0.5*n
    for _ in range(100):
        d=t-h.sum()
        if abs(d)<1e-15: break
        if d>0:
            r=(1-h).sum(); h += d/r*(1-h) if r>1e-15 else 0
        else:
            s=h.sum(); h += d/s*h if s>1e-15 else 0
    h=np.clip(h,0,1); d=t-h.sum()
    if abs(d)>1e-13:
        i=int(np.argmax((1-h)*(h>1e-8)+(h>1e-8)?))? simpler:
        if d>0: i=int(np.argmax(1-h)); h[i]=min(1,h[i]+d)
        else: i=int(np.argmax(h)); h[i]=max(0,h[i]+d)
    return h
```
Need avoid line `h += ... if r>... else 0` ambiguous? `h += d/r*(1-h) if r>1e-12 else 0.0` adds scalar 0.0; okay. But if r zero, 1-h maybe zero. Use explicit.

```

def continuous_local(...):
 bestc=...
 for it in range(max_iters):
  ...
  active=np.argsort(C)[-L:]
  Aact=A[:,active]; Cact=C[active]
  cand=[]
  for i in range(n):
   room=1-h[i]
   if room<=1e-12: continue
   js=np.flatnonzero(h > h[i]+1e-10)
   if js.size==0: continue
   D=np.minimum(room, h[js]); msk=D>1e-10; js=js[msk]; D=D[msk]
   if js.size==0: continue
   B=Aact[:,i]-Aact[:,js]
   sc=np.max(Cact + B*np.minimum(0.05,D)[None,:],axis=0)
   o=np.argsort(sc)[:3]
   for t in o: cand.append((float(sc[t]),i,int(js[t]),float(D[t])))
  cand.sort()
  found=False
  for _,i,j,D in cand:
    if time... break
    bestv=maxc; bestd=0
    for d in (D,D*0.5,D*0.25,D*0.125,min(0.025,D),min(0.005,D)):
       if d<=... continue
       v=eval_pair_move(...); if v<bestv-1e-8: ...
    if bestd:
       apply_pair(...); found=True; break
  if not found: break
 return bestc
```

`active_lp_polish` compact. Use local variables. L_values from n.
```
def active_lp_polish(h,n,deadline,max_iters=12):
 if linprog is None: return h, eval_c5(h,n)
 h=project_feasible(h,n); dx=2/n; best=eval_c5(h,n); C=...; A=...
 m=2*n-1; Ls=(40,80,160)
 if n<=550: Ls=Ls+(m,)
 elif m<400: Ls=Ls+(m,)
 else: Ls=Ls+(300,)
 for L in Ls:
  L=min(L,m)
  for _ in range(max_iters):
   if time.time()>deadline: return h,best
   act=np.argsort(C)[-L:]
   Arows=A[:,act].T
   try:
      A_ub=np.column_stack((Arows, -np.ones(L)))
      A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1
      bounds=[(-float(v),1-float(v)) for v in h]+[(-1000.,0.)]
      res=linprog(np.r_[np.zeros(n),1], A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=np.zeros(L), A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=0,bounds=bounds,method='highs')
   except Exception: break
   if res is None or res.status!=0: break
   t=float(res.x[-1])
   if t>-1e-7: break
   v=res.x[:n].astype(float)
   if np.linalg.norm(v)<1e-11: break
   lm=0.999
   p=v>1e-12; n=v<-1e-12
   if p.any(): lm=min(lm,float(np.min((1-h[p]-1e-12)/v[p])))
   if n.any(): lm=min(lm,float(np.min((h[n]-1e-12)/(-v[n]))))
   if lm<=1e-6: break
   lams=np.linspace(0,lm,33)[1:]
   maxc=float(C.max()); bl=0; bv=maxc; bh=None
   for a in lams:
      hn=h+a*v
      if hn.min()<0 or hn.max()>1: hn=project_feasible(hn,n)
      val=float(compute_C_ours(hn,n).max())
      if val<bv-1e-8: bv=val; bl=a; bh=hn
   if bh is not None:
      h=project_feasible(bh,n); C=compute_C_ours(h,n); A=build_A(h,n)
      best=eval_c5(h,n)
   else: break
 return h,best
```
Bounds list comprehension uses float(v) for each h. If h_i negative small, lower positive? project should nonnegative. If upper<lower due nan? no.
Use variable `n` conflict with boolean neg array? I used `n=v<-1e-12` shadows n length! Bad. Use `negm`.

`column_stack` with Arows (L,n) and -ones (L,) works? `np.column_stack((Arows, -np.ones(L)))` yields (L,n+1). Good.
`np.r_[np.zeros(n),1]` c length n+1.

      c2=eval; update

   if c2<best_c: best_h,c2? best_c=c2; best_n? active_lp returns same n.

`sc = np.max(Cact + B * ds[None, :], axis=0)`. If J=1, sc shape (1,). Good.
`order=np.argsort(sc)[:min(3, len(sc))]`.

In binary_local, if zero or one arrays empty, break. If h has non-binary but zeros/ones threshold, d=1 may leave h near. Fine.

Potential issue in binary_local after periodic recompute: `C=compute_C_ours(h,n); A=build_A(h,n)`; but `W` still same size. The local variable `C` reassigned, but in candidate loop next sweep uses new C. Good. `bestc` reset.

`binary_local` if no improvement breaks. If max_sweeps 150 but time slice, okay.

Now, final check of imports: We need scipy? We import linprog. If no linprog, fine. We don't import scipy.signal; use np.correlate. Good.

Let's write final code. Ensure fence exactly.
</think>

```python
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def eval_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * len(h)
    for _ in range(120):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0.0:
            i = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            h[i] = min(1.0, h[i] + diff)
        else:
            i = int(np.argmax(h))
            h[i] = max(0.0, h[i] + diff)
    return h


def compute_C_ours(h, n):
    m = 2 * n - 1
    base = n - 1
    C = np.zeros(m)
    C[base] = np.dot(h, 1.0 - h)
    for k in range(1, n):
        nv = n - k
        C[base + k] = np.dot(h[:nv], 1.0 - h[k:k + nv])
        C[base - k] = np.dot(h[k:], 1.0 - h[:nv])
    return C


def build_A(h, n):
    m = 2 * n - 1
    base = n - 1
    A = np.zeros((n, m))
    A[:, base] = 1.0 - 2.0 * h
    for k in range(1, n):
        nv = n - k
        ip = base + k
        ineg = base - k
        A[:nv, ip] += 1.0 - h[k:k + nv]
        A[kk:, ip] -= h[:nv]
        A[kk:, ineg] += 1.0 - h[:nv]
        A[:nv, ineg] -= h[kk:]
    return A


def update_A_change(A, q, delta, rows, base):
    if delta == 0.0:
        return
    A[rows, base + q - rows] -= delta
    A[rows, base + rows - q] -= delta


def apply_pair(C, A, h, i, j, d, rows, base):
    if abs(d) < 1e-15:
        return float(C.max())
    C += d * A[i]
    C -= d * A[j]
    d2 = d * d
    C[base] += -2.0 * d2
    if i != j:
        C[base + j - i] += d2
        C[base + i - j] += d2
    hi = float(h[i]) + d
    hj = float(h[j]) - d
    if -1e-10 < hi < 0.0:
        hi = 0.0
    elif 1.0 < hi < 1.0 + 1e-10:
        hi = 1.0
    if -1e-10 < hj < 0.0:
        hj = 0.0
    elif 1.0 < hj < 1.0 + 1e-10:
        hj = 1.0
    h[i] = hi
    h[j] = hj
    update_A_change(A, i, d, rows, base)
    update_A_change(A, j, -d, rows, base)
    return float(C.max())


def eval_pair_move(C, W, A, i, j, d, base):
    np.copyto(W, C)
    if d == 1.0:
        W += A[i]
        W -= A[j]
    else:
        W += d * A[i]
        W -= d * A[j]
    d2 = d * d
    W[base] += -2.0 * d2
    if i != j:
        W[base + j - i] += d2
        W[base + i - j] += d2
    return float(W.max())


def make_binary(h, n):
    b = np.zeros(n)
    k = n // 2
    if k > 0:
        idx = np.argsort(h, kind="mergesort")[::-1][:k]
        b[idx] = 1.0
    return b


def make_random_binary(n, rng):
    h = np.zeros(n)
    k = n // 2
    if k > 0:
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False)
        h[idx] = 1.0
    return h


def binary_local(C, A, h, n, rows, base, W, deadline, max_sweeps=100):
    bestc = float(C.max())
    sweeps = 0
    improved = True
    accepted = 0
    while improved and sweeps < max_sweeps and time.time() < deadline:
        improved = False
        sweeps += 1
        zeros = np.flatnonzero(h <= 1e-9)
        ones = np.flatnonzero(h >= 1.0 - 1e-9)
        if zeros.size == 0 or ones.size == 0:
            break
        bi = -1
        bj = -1
        bv = bestc
        for i in zeros:
            i = int(i)
            Ai = A[i]
            for j in ones:
                j = int(j)
                np.copyto(W, C)
                W += Ai
                W -= A[j]
                W[base] += -2.0
                W[base + j - i] += 1.0
                W[base + i - j] += 1.0
                v = float(W.max())
                if v < bv - 1e-9:
                    bv = v
                    bi = i
                    bj = j
        if bi >= 0:
            bestc = apply_pair(C, A, h, bi, bj, 1.0, rows, base)
            accepted += 1
            if accepted % 25 == 0:
                C = compute_C_ours(h, n)
                A = build_A(h, n)
                bestc = float(C.max())
            improved = True
    return bestc


def continuous_local(C, A, h, n, rows, base, W, deadline, max_iters=100, L=80):
    bestc = float(C.max())
    accepted = 0
    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        maxc = float(C.max())
        if maxc < bestc - 1e-12:
            bestc = maxc
        Luse = min(L, 2 * n - 1)
        active = np.argsort(C)[-Luse:]
        Aact = A[:, active]
        Cact = C[active]
        cand = []
        for i in range(n):
            room = 1.0 - h[i]
            if room <= 1e-12:
                continue
            js = np.flatnonzero(h > h[i] + 1e-10)
            if js.size == 0:
                continue
            D = np.minimum(room, h[js])
            msk = D > 1e-10
            js = js[msk]
            D = D[msk]
            if js.size == 0:
                continue
            ds = np.minimum(0.05, D)
            B = Aact[:, i] - Aact[:, js]
            sc = np.max(Cact + B * ds[None, :], axis=0)
            order = np.argsort(sc)[:min(3, len(sc))]
            for t in order:
                cand.append((float(sc[t]), int(i), int(js[t]), float(D[t])))
        if not cand:
            break
        cand.sort()
        found = False
        tested = 0
        for _, i, j, Dv in cand:
            if time.time() > deadline or tested >= 160:
                break
            tested += 1
            bestv = maxc
            bestd = 0.0
            for d in (Dv, 0.5 * Dv, 0.25 * Dv, 0.125 * Dv, min(0.025, Dv), min(1e-3, Dv)):
                if d <= 1e-12 or d > Dv + 1e-12:
                    continue
                v = eval_pair_move(C, W, A, i, j, float(d), base)
                if v < bestv - 1e-8:
                    bestv = v
                    bestd = float(d)
            if bestd > 0.0:
                apply_pair(C, A, h, i, j, bestd, rows, base)
                accepted += 1
                if accepted % 15 == 0:
                    C = compute_C_ours(h, n)
                    A = build_A(h, n)
                    bestc = float(C.max())
                else:
                    bestc = float(C.max())
                found = True
                break
        if not found:
            break
    return bestc


def active_lp_polish(h, n, deadline, max_iters=12):
    if linprog is None:
        return h, eval_c5(h, n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best = eval_c5(h, n)
    C = compute_C_ours(h, n)
    A = build_A(h, n)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    Ls = (40, 80, 160)
    if n <= 550 and m > 160:
        Ls = Ls + (m,)
    elif 160 < m < 400:
        Ls = Ls + (m,)
    elif m >= 400:
        Ls = Ls + (400,)
    for L in Ls:
        Luse = min(int(L), m)
        for _ in range(max_iters):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                return h, best
            active = np.argsort(C)[-Luse:]
            Arows = A[:, active].T
            try:
                A_ub = np.column_stack((Arows, -np.ones(Luse)))
                A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1))
                A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
                bounds = []
                for vv in h:
                    lo = -float(vv)
                    hi = 1.0 - float(vv)
                    if hi < lo:
                        hi = lo
                    bounds.append((lo, hi))
                bounds.append((-1000.0, 0.0))
                cc = np.zeros(n + 1)
                cc[-1] = 1.0
                res = linprog(cc, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=np.zeros(Luse), A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=0.0,
                              bounds=bounds, method="highs")
            except Exception:
                break
            if res is None or getattr(res, "status", 1) != 0:
                break
            tval = float(res.x[-1])
            if tval > -1e-7:
                break
            v = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float)
            if float(np.linalg.norm(v)) < 1e-11:
                break
            lam_max = 0.999
            posm = v > 1e-12
            negm = v < -1e-12
            if np.any(posm):
                lam_max = min(lam_max, float(np.min((1.0 - h[posm] - 1e-12) / v[posm])))
            if np.any(negm):
                lam_max = min(lam_max, float(np.min((h[negm] - 1e-12) / (-v[negm]))))
            if lam_max <= 1e-6:
                break
            maxc = float(C.max())
            blam = 0.0
            bval = maxc
            bh = None
            lams = np.linspace(0.0, lam_max, 33)[1:]
            for a in lams:
                hn = h + float(a) * v
                if hn.min() < -1e-12 or hn.max() > 1.0 + 1e-12:
                    hn = project_feasible(hn, n)
                val = float(compute_C_ours(hn, n).max())
                if val < bval - 1e-8:
                    bval = val
                    blam = float(a)
                    bh = hn.copy()
            if bh is not None:
                h = project_feasible(bh, n)
                C = compute_C_ours(h, n)
                A = build_A(h, n)
                best = eval_c5(h, n)
            else:
                break
    return h, best


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    final_end = t0 + total - 4.0
    main_end = t0 + 0.60 * total
    alt_end = t0 + 0.88 * total
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = int(len(h))
        if n < 2:
            raise ValueError
    except Exception:
        n = 500
        h = make_random_binary(n, rng)

    if n % 2 == 1:
        h = h[:-1]
        n -= 1
    if n < 2:
        n = 500
        h = make_random_binary(n, rng)

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_n = n
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h, best_n)

    if linprog is not None and best_n <= 900 and time.time() < main_end:
        h2, c2 = active_lp_polish(best_h, best_n, min(main_end, t0 + 0.35 * total), 12)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    if best_n <= 900 and time.time() < main_end:
        hw = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
        C = compute_C_ours(hw, best_n)
        A = build_A(hw, best_n)
        rows = np.arange(best_n)
        base = best_n - 1
        W = np.zeros(2 * best_n - 1)
        continuous_local(C, A, hw, best_n, rows, base, W, min(main_end, final_end), 100, 80)
        h2 = project_feasible(hw, best_n)
        c2 = eval_c5(h2, best_n)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    if best_n <= 700 and time.time() < main_end:
        hb = make_binary(best_h, best_n)
        C = compute_C_ours(hb, best_n)
        A = build_A(hb, best_n)
        rows = np.arange(best_n)
        base = best_n - 1
        W = np.zeros(2 * best_n - 1)
        ms = 200 if best_n <= 250 else 100 if best_n <= 500 else 60
        binary_local(C, A, hb, best_n, rows, base, W, min(main_end, final_end), ms)
        h2 = project_feasible(hb, best_n)
        c2 = eval_c5(h2, best_n)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    for n2 in (100, 150, 200, 250, 300, 400, 600, 800):
        if time.time() > alt_end - 50:
            break
        if n2 % 2 != 0 or n2 < 50 or n2 > 900 or n2 == best_n:
            continue
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, best_n, endpoint=False)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n2, endpoint=False)
        h2 = project_feasible(np.interp(xn, xo, best_h), n2)
        if n2 <= 400 and time.time() < alt_end - 30:
            hb = make_binary(h2, n2)
            C = compute_C_ours(hb, n2)
            A = build_A(hb, n2)
            rows = np.arange(n2)
            base = n2 - 1
            W = np.zeros(2 * n2 - 1)
            alloc = 40.0 if n2 <= 200 else 14.0
            deadline2 = min(alt_end, time.time() + alloc)
            binary_local(C, A, hb, n2, rows, base, W, deadline2, 300 if n2 <= 200 else 90)
            c2 = eval_c5(project_feasible(hb, n2), n2)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_h = project_feasible(hb, n2).copy()
                best_c = c2
                best_n = n2
        if n2 <= 250 and time.time() < alt_end - 25:
            hr = make_random_binary(n2, rng)
            C = compute_C_ours(hr, n2)
            A = build_A(hr, n2)
            rows = np.arange(n2)
            base = n2 - 1
            W = np.zeros(2 * n2 - 1)
            binary_local(C, A, hr, n2, rows, base, W, min(alt_end, time.time() + 10.0), 400)
            c2 = eval_c5(project_feasible(hr, n2), n2)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_h = project_feasible(hr, n2).copy()
                best_c = c2
                best_n = n2
        if time.time() < alt_end - 20 and n2 <= 800:
            hc = project_feasible(h2, n2)
            C = compute_C_ours(hc, n2)
            A = build_A(hc, n2)
            rows = np.arange(n2)
            base = n2 - 1
            W = np.zeros(2 * n2 - 1)
            alloc = 30.0 if n2 > 500 else 12.0
            dl2 = min(alt_end, time.time() + alloc)
            continuous_local(C, A, hc, n2, rows, base, W, dl2, 45, 64)
            c2 = eval_c5(project_feasible(hc, n2), n2)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_h = project_feasible(hc, n2).copy()
                best_c = c2
                best_n = n2

    if time.time() < final_end - 25 and best_n <= 900:
        h2, c2 = active_lp_polish(best_h, best_n, final_end - 6.0, 8)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    if time.time() < final_end - 8 and best_n <= 900:
        hw = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
        C = compute_C_ours(hw, best_n)
        A = build_A(hw, best_n)
        rows = np.arange(best_n)
        base = best_n - 1
        W = np.zeros(2 * best_n - 1)
        continuous_local(C, A, hw, best_n, rows, base, W, final_end - 2.0, 30, 64)
        h2 = project_feasible(hw, best_n)
        c2 = eval_c5(h2, best_n)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, best_c, best_n
```
